## `AFG-4722-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Role:** Raman-Spectroscopy Metrologist

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Draft-Beer-Regulator Mechanic
- **Scenario:** In-situ Removal, Reassembly & Dynamic Tolerance Reset SOP for Wine Cellar Split HVAC & Backup Chiller Wear Components
